## 적용 완료 내역

### 씬 오브젝트 (기존 크기 유지, Visual 자식으로 FBX 추가)
| 씬 오브젝트 | FBX 모델 | 크기 |
|---|---|---|
| `ConverterMachine` | convey | 2.65 × 2.4 × 2.0 |
| `PoliceDeskArea` | desk | 1.5 × 1.8 × 2.5 |
| `PrisonerSpawner` | gate | 2.0 × 2.0 × 2.0 |
| `Prison_20` | jail1 | 8.1 × 3.1 × 10.2 |
| `Player` | playcharacter | 1.05 × 2.5 × 1.02 |
| `OreDropZone` | plate | 플레이트 비주얼 |
| `HandcuffPickupZone` | plate | 수갑 쌓이는 트레이 |
| `MoneyPickupZone` | money | 돈 비주얼 |
| `OuterWalls` (18개 세그먼트) | fence | 각 벽 크기 맞춤 |
| `MiningGridGroup` (35개 포스트/레일) | fence | 각 구간 크기 맞춤 |

### 프리팹 업데이트
- `RockPrefab` → rock FBX
- `HandcuffPrefab` → handcuff FBX
- `CashPrefab` → money FBX
- `WorkerNPCPrefab`, `AutoSellNPCPrefab` → miner FBX
- `PrisonerPrefab` → prisoner_before FBX + prisoner_after 완납 시 자동 교체

### prisoner_before/after 연동
- `PrisonerAI.SwapToAfterVisual()` 추가 — 수갑 완납 시 prisoner_after 모델로 자동 교체
- `PrisonerAfterVisualPrefab` 생성 후 PrisonerPrefab에 자동 연결

### 재사용 가능 에디터 툴
`Tools > Apply FBX Designs` 메뉴 — 모델 추가/변경 시 단계별로 재실행 가능

[사용자]
This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested applying 3D FBX models from `./modeling/object/` to the Unity scene, replacing existing placeholder objects. Specific requirements:
   - Use textures from each FBX's own subfolder
   - Match the exact size/bounds of the original objects being replaced
   - Delete old objects after placing new FBX-based ones
   - Modify collision shapes/ranges as needed
   - Mapping: convey→ConverterMachine, desk→PoliceDeskArea, fence→OuterWalls+MiningGridGroup, gate→PrisonerSpawner, handcuff→HandcuffPrefab, jail1→Prison_20, miner→WorkerNPCPrefab/AutoSellNPCPrefab, money→CashPrefab+MoneyPickupZone, plate→OreDropZone+HandcuffPickupZone, playcharacter→Player, prisoner_before→PrisonerPrefab, prisoner_after→post-handcuff state, rock→RockPrefab
   
   Second request: "새로 바뀐 object들의 size가 안맞아" (newly changed objects' sizes don't match) — fix incorrect visual sizes.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unity Editor scripting with `[MenuItem]`, `EditorApplication.ExecuteMenuItem`
   - FBX import with ModelImporter (globalScale=100, fileScale=0.01 for cm-unit models)
   - URP Lit materials with _BaseMap, _BumpMap, _MetallicGlossMap
   - PrefabUtility.LoadPrefabContents / SaveAsPrefabAsset / UnloadPrefabContents
   - Unity Renderer.bounds (world-space AABB)
   - FBX baked 270°X rotation (Z-up → Y-up Blender export convention)
   - world/local axis mapping with -90°X rotation: worldX=localX, worldY=localZ, worldZ=localY
   - ScaleNonUniformBaked formula: localScale = (tW.x/b.x, tW.z/b.z, tW.y/b.y)
   - ScaleByHeight for characters (Y-axis uniform scale)
   - PrisonerAI state machine and visual swapping
   - SerializedObject for setting private inspector fields

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`Assets/Editor/FBXDesignApplicator.cs`** (NEW — main editor tool)
     - 4-step menu tool: `Tools/Apply FBX Designs`
     - Step 1: Copies FBX+textures from `modeling/object/` → `Assets/Models/`
     - Step 2: Creates URP Lit materials with diffuse/normal/metallic textures
     - Step 3: Replaces scene objects with FBX visuals
     - Step 4: Updates prefabs
     - Key functions: `AddFBXVisual(parent, fbxPath, matName, targetBounds, localOffset, uniform, preserveFBXRot=true)`, `ScaleNonUniformBaked`, `ScaleUniformBaked`, `ScaleByHeight`, `ScaleFenceSegment`, `ScaleNonUniform`, `RemoveChildrenExcept`, `EnsureBoxCollider`
     - Current state of `AddFBXVisual`:
     ```csharp
     static void AddFBXVisual(GameObject parent, string fbxPath, string matName,
                              Vector3 targetBounds, Vector3 localOffset, bool uniform,
                              bool preserveFBXRot = true)
     {
         var fbx = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(fbxPath);
         if (fbx == null) { Debug.LogWarning("[FBXDesigner] FBX not found: " + fbxPath); return; }
         var existingVis = parent.transform.Find("Visual");
         if (existingVis != null) Object.DestroyImmediate(existingVis.gameObject);
         var vis = (GameObject)Object.Instantiate(fbx, parent.transform);
         vis.name = "Visual";
         vis.transform.localPosition = localOffset;
         if (!preserveFBXRot)
             vis.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
         var mat = GetMat(matName);
         if (mat != null)
             foreach (var r in vis.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>())
                 r.sharedMaterial = mat;
         if (preserveFBXRot)
         {
             if (uniform) ScaleUniformBaked(vis, targetBounds);
             else ScaleNonUniformBaked(vis, targetBounds);
         }
         else
         {
             if (uniform) ScaleNonUniform(vis, targetBounds);
             else ScaleNonUniform(vis, targetBounds);
         }
     }
     ```
     - Current `ScaleNonUniformBaked`:
     ```csharp
     // FBX baked 270°X (Z-up) 회전용 비균등 스케일.
     // Unity: world X = local X,  world Y = local Z,  world Z = local Y
     // → b.size.x = naturalLocalX, b.size.y = naturalLocalZ, b.size.z = naturalLocalY
     // localScale: (tW.x/b.x, tW.z/b.z, tW.y/b.y)
     static void ScaleNonUniformBaked(GameObject go, Vector3 targetWorldSize)
     {
         go.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
         var renderers = go.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>();
         if (renderers.Length == 0) return;
         var b = renderers[0].bounds;
         foreach (var r in renderers) b.Encapsulate(r.bounds);
         if (b.size.x < 0.001f || b.size.y < 0.001f || b.size.z < 0.001f) return;
         go.transform.localScale = new Vector3(
             targetWorldSize.x / b.size.x,
             targetWorldSize.z / b.size.z,   // worldZ target → local Y scale
             targetWorldSize.y / b.size.y);  // worldY target → local Z scale
     }
     ```
     - Current `ScaleByHeight`:
     ```csharp
     static void ScaleByHeight(GameObject go, float targetHeight)
     {
         go.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
         var renderers = go.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>();
         if (renderers.Length == 0) return;
         var b = renderers[0].bounds;
         foreach (var r in renderers) b.Encapsulate(r.bounds);
         if (b.size.y < 0.001f) return;
         go.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * (targetHeight / b.size.y);
     }
     ```
     - Current `ScaleFenceSegment`:
     ```csharp
     static void ScaleFenceSegment(GameObject go, Vector3 wallLocalSize)
     {
         go.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
         var renderers = go.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>();
         if (renderers.Length == 0) return;
         var b = renderers[0].bounds;
         foreach (var r in renderers) b.Encapsulate(r.bounds);
         if (b.size.x < 0.001f || b.size.y < 0.001f) return;
         float wallLength = Mathf.Max(wallLocalSize.x, wallLocalSize.z);
         float wallHeight = Mathf.Max(wallLocalSize.y, 1.0f);
         // baked: world X = fence width, world Y = fence height
         float sx = (wallLength > 0.1f) ? wallLength / b.size.x : 1.0f;
         float sy_world = wallHeight / b.size.y;
         // localScale Z → world Y (baked 270°X)
         go.transform.localScale = new Vector3(sx, 1.0f, sy_world);
     }
     ```

   - **`Assets/Scripts/NPC/PrisonerAI.cs`** (MODIFIED)
     - Added `[SerializeField] private GameObject afterVisualPrefab;` field
     - Added `SwapToAfterVisual()` method called from `ReceiveHandcuff()` when handcuffs complete
     - SwapToAfterVisual hides "Visual" child and instantiates afterVisualPrefab as "Visual_After"
     ```csharp
     private void SwapToAfterVisual()
     {
         var before = transform.Find("Visual");
         if (before != null) before.gameObject.SetActive(false);
         if (afterVisualPrefab != null)
         {
             var after = Instantiate(afterVisualPrefab, transform);
             after.name = "Visual_After";
             after.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;
             after.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
             if (before != null) after.transform.localScale = before.localScale;
         }
     }
     ```

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **FBX scale too tiny (cm units)**: FBX models had fileScale=0.01 (cm), globalScale=1 → models appeared ~100x too small. Fixed by setting `globalScale=100` via ModelImporter for all standard models.
   - **localRotation=Quaternion.identity breaks Z-up FBX**: Setting identity rotation removes the baked 270°X rotation correction, causing Y and Z world bounds to swap. Fixed by adding `preserveFBXRot` parameter — when true, don't override localRotation.
   - **Player too small (0.91m)**: ScaleUniform using Mathf.Min chose scale=1.02 (Z was already at target). Fixed by adding `ScaleByHeight(vis, 2.0f)` which scales uniformly based on Y dimension → player now 2.0m tall.
   - **ScaleNonUniform wrong for baked-rotation FBX**: With baked 270°X rotation, world Y = local Z, world Z = local Y. Old formula `(tX/bX, tY/bY, tZ/bZ)` applied target Y to localScale Y, but localScale Y controls world Z. Fixed with `ScaleNonUniformBaked`: `(tX/bX, tZ/bZ, tY/bY)` — targetZ controls scaleY (→worldZ), targetY controls scaleZ (→worldY).
   - **Script not compiling after edit**: Used `AssetDatabase.Refresh(ImportAssetOptions.ForceUpdate)` and waited for `EditorApplication.isCompiling` to return false before re-executing.
   - **PrisonerAI private field reference**: Used SerializedObject to set `afterVisualPrefab` private serialized field in prefab editing scope.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **FBX natural bounds measurement**: Discovered that FBX models instantiated with `localRotation=identity` give different world bounds than with baked rotation. Measured both via unity-cli exec to understand the Y/Z axis swap.
   - **ScaleNonUniformBaked derivation**: Worked through the -90°X rotation math (world Y = local Z * scaleZ, world Z = local Y * scaleY) to derive correct formula: `localScale = (tW.x/b.x, tW.z/b.z, tW.y/b.y)` where b is measured with baked rotation active.
   - **Fence scaling approach**: Changed from ScaleNonUniform (which caused Z-stretch of 8-10x on thin fence panels) to ScaleFenceSegment which scales X (length) and uses scaleZ for world Y (height), keeping natural local Y.
   - **Money zone visual**: Used ScaleByHeight(0.5f) directly since money FBX is very thin in Z (0.145m natural) making ScaleNonUniformBaked produce extreme Z-stretch for 1.0m target.
   - **ONGOING**: After implementing ScaleNonUniformBaked and re-running Step 3, ConverterMachine/PoliceDeskArea/PrisonerSpawner/Prison_20 still show wrong sizes matching OLD ScaleNonUniform formula. Investigation suggests possible compilation cache issue.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unity `ModelImporter`: `globalScale`, `useFileScale`, `bakeAxisConversion`, `importAnimation`, `animationType`
   - Blender Z-up → Unity Y-up axis correction via `bakeAxisConversion = true`
   - FBX unit scale: Blender exports in cm (fileScale=0.01), so `globalScale=100` is WRONG (produces 190m objects); correct is `globalScale=1` — fileScale handles the cm→m conversion internally
   - Tripo AI character models export at normalized 1.0m height → `globalScale=1.8` to reach game-appropriate 1.8m
   - URP Lit shader: `_BaseMap` (diffuse), `_BumpMap` (normal), `_MetallicGlossMap` (metallic)
   - Two FBX types:
     - **base.fbx**: Blender props, Z-up, cm units → `globalScale=1`, `bakeAxisConversion=true`, no animation
     - **tripo_convert_*.fbx**: Character models from Tripo AI, Y-up, m units → `globalScale=1.8`, `bakeAxisConversion=true`, Generic rig
   - `.fbm` folder: texture folder auto-created by FBX exporter alongside tripo character FBX files
   - `Renderer.bounds` in edit mode for measuring model world-space sizes
   - Prison scene structure: `Prison_20` at `(3, 0, -7)`, children `Floor/WallBack/WallFront/WallRear/Label`, front wall at localZ=+5 (entrance faces +Z)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`Assets/Editor/FBXModelImporter.cs`** (NEW)
     - Editor tool with `[MenuItem("Tools/FBX Model Importer/Import All Models")]` and `[MenuItem("Tools/FBX Model Importer/Reconfigure Scale Only")]`
     - Copies FBX + textures from `modeling/object/{name}/` to `Assets/Models/{name}/`
     - `ConfigureFBX()`: sets globalScale, bakeAxisConversion, importAnimation, addCollider=false, SaveAndReimport
     - `CreateMaterial()`: creates URP Lit material in `Assets/Models/Materials/{name}_mat.mat`
     - `SetTex()`: assigns diffuse/normal/metallic textures; sets NormalMap importer type for `_BumpMap`
     - `FindFBX()`: finds `base.fbx` for props, `tripo_convert_*.fbx` for characters
     - `FindFBMTexture()`: finds .jpg in `.fbm` subfolder (had path bug; fixed manually)
     - Model definitions table:
     ```csharp
     new ModelDef("convey",          "base.fbx",   1f, false, "컨베이어 머신"),
     ```

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **globalScale=100 caused 190m objects**: Initially used `globalScale=100` thinking models were in cm. But `fileScale=0.01` already handles cm→m conversion; `globalScale=100` additionally multiplied × 100 = 190m. Fixed by changing all prop globalScale to `1f`.

   - `PrefabUtility.LoadPrefabContents` / `SaveAsPrefabAsset` for prefab editing

   - **`Assets/Scripts/Interactables/DropZone.cs`**
     - `DeliverOre` replaced with coroutine-based `OreDropAndFly`:
     ```csharp
     private void DeliverOre(PlayerStackManager stackManager)
     {
         if (!stackManager.HasItemOfType(ItemType.Ore)) return;
         if (converterMachine == null) return;
         StackItem ore = stackManager.RemoveTopItemOfType(ItemType.Ore);
         if (ore == null) return;
         StartCoroutine(OreDropAndFly(ore.gameObject));
     }
     private System.Collections.IEnumerator OreDropAndFly(GameObject oreObj)
     {
         PushItem(oreObj);
         yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
         if (oreObj == null) yield break;
         items.Remove(oreObj);
         Vector3 startPos = oreObj.transform.position;
         oreObj.transform.SetParent(null);
         oreObj.transform.position = startPos;
         Vector3 endPos = converterMachine.transform.position + Vector3.up * 0.5f;
         float duration = 0.35f; float elapsed = 0f;
         while (elapsed < duration && oreObj != null) {
             elapsed += Time.deltaTime;
             float t = Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / duration);
             Vector3 pos = Vector3.Lerp(startPos, endPos, t);
             pos.y += Mathf.Sin(t * Mathf.PI) * 0.8f;
             oreObj.transform.position = pos;
             yield return null;
         }
         if (oreObj != null) { converterMachine.ReceiveOre(1); Destroy(oreObj); }
         for (int i = 0; i < items.Count; i++)
             if (items[i] != null)
                 items[i].transform.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, i * stackSpacingY, 0f);
     }
     ```

     ```csharp
     void Update()

     }
     ```

### 2. 자동 판매 속도
- `AutoSellNPC` 수갑 전달 루프에 `GameSettings.dropInterval`(0.3초) 간격 추가 → 플레이어가 드롭하는 것과 동일한 속도로 한 개씩 전달

### 3. 아이템 스택 위치
- `PlayerStackManager.GetLocalStackPosition()` 수정
  - 돌(Ore): X = -0.28 (왼쪽)
  - 수갑(Handcuff): X = 0 (중앙)  
  - 돈(Cash): X = +0.28 (오른쪽)
  - 각 타입 내에서 높이 방향으로 쌓임

### 4. 자동 채굴 방향
- `WorkerNPC` 완전 재작성: `assignedRow`(Z행 고정, X방향 이동) → `assignedCol`(X열 고정, Z방향 20칸 왕복)
- `UpgradeZone.SpawnWorkers()`: row 0,3,6 → col 4,5,6 (7열 중 아래 3열)
- `MiningGrid.GetNodeWorldPos(col, row)` 추가
- `DropZone.WorkerDeliverOre()` 추가: OreDropZone에 광석 시각적 스태킹 + 변환기 자동 전달, 변환 완료 후 정리

### 1. 돈 Spawn 애니메이션
- 수감자 머리 위에서 돈 오브젝트 생성
- `moneyFlySpeed`(8 단위/초) + `moneyArcHeight`(1.5) 포물선으로 MoneyPickupZone까지 날아감
- 도착 후 `PushItem()`으로 스택에 편입 (Inspector에서 속도/높이 조절 가능)

### 2. MarkerVis 기반 경로
- `GetMarkerPos(Transform t)` 패턴: 마커에 `MarkerVis` 자식이 있으면 그 위치 사용, 없으면 부모 위치 fallback
- **PrisonerSpawner**: spawn/wait/desk 모두 MarkerVis 반영
- **CellManager**: `PrisonEntrancePos` = OutsideQueueStart의 MarkerVis (-9.96, 0, -7), `CellGridOrigin` = CellRoot의 MarkerVis (3, 0, -7.21)
- 앞으로 씬에서 MarkerVis만 이동하면 경로가 자동으로 따라감

### 3. 감옥 내 수감자 위치
- 셀 슬롯 y = **0.85** (캡슐 반높이) → 수감자가 바닥에 정확히 서도록
- 그리드 원점을 `CellRoot.MarkerVis (3, 0, -7.21)`로 변경 → 실제 감옥 영역과 정렬
- 슬롯 방향을 `+Z` 방향으로 통일 (이전 `-row*1.5` → `+row*1.5`)

|---|---|---|

|---|---|---|

| `struct_desk.fbx` | 경찰 책상 | ~1.8 × 0.9 × 0.8 |

---

### 주의사항

- 모든 FBX는 **Y-up, Z-forward** (Unity 기본 방향)
- **Pivot 위치**: 캐릭터는 발 아래, 나머지는 오브젝트 중심 또는 바닥 중심
- 텍스처는 별도 이미지 파일 (PNG)로 제공하거나 **SubMesh 분리**로 주면 머티리얼 자동 분리 가능
- `struct_prison.fbx`는 철창 부분을 별도 SubMesh로 분리해주면 반투명 처리 적용 용이

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user is developing a Unity mobile game (Prison Life / SupercentAssignment) — a top-down police/prison management game. Requests across the conversation:
   - Implement auto-sell and auto-mine upgrades that spawn player-lookalike NPCs
   - Fix prisoner movement to L-shaped path (wait→desk→prisonwait→prison)
   - Match AutoSellNPC deposit speed to player's dropInterval
   - Differentiate stack positions by item type (ore/handcuff/cash)
   - Change WorkerNPC to traverse 20 rows along assigned column (bottom 3 cols: 4,5,6)
   - Money should fly from prisoner to money zone with arc animation
   - All prisoner waypoints use MarkerVis child positions
   - Fix prisoner Y position in cells (capsule half-height 0.85)
   - Replace OreDropZone/HandcuffPickupZone visuals with dashed floor outlines
   - Add interactive dashed outlines to OreDropZone and DeskZone (white→green on enter)
   - Apply PNG design assets to upgrade zones and HUD
   - **Final request**: Change upgrade zone image quads from billboard (camera-facing) to floor-facing (flat on floor, facing upward)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unity URP (Universal Render Pipeline) - Unlit/Lit shaders
   - CameraBillboard component (rotates to face camera each LateUpdate)
   - World-space UI (TextMeshPro 3D, Quads)
   - Screen-space Canvas UI (TextMeshProUGUI, Image, Button)
   - SerializedObject/SerializedProperty for editor-time property assignment
   - Unity Trigger system (OnTriggerEnter/Exit) for zone detection
   - Coroutines for movement, animations, routines
   - UpgradeZone fill bar (Y-scale grows from 0 to maxFillHeight)
   - MarkerVis pattern: child GameObject used as actual waypoint position
   - unity-cli exec for runtime/editor C# execution
   - Git workflow: stage → commit → push

3. Files and Code Sections:

     ```csharp
         float t = 0f;

             flat.y += moneyArcHeight * Mathf.Sin(t * Mathf.PI);
     ```

4. Errors and Fixes:

   - **MaxIndicator not visible**: Missing `CameraBillboard` component. All other 3D labels use `MakeLabel()` which auto-adds it, but MaxIndicator was created manually. Fixed by adding CameraBillboard to both scene YAML and SceneBuilder.cs.

   - **TMP font material/atlas null**: `MalgunGothic SDF.asset` had `atlasTextures[0] = null` and `material = null`. Caused `UnassignedReferenceException` on all TMP text. Fixed by calling `TMP_FontAsset.CreateFontAsset()` with MalgunGothic.ttf, then assigning font asset to all 21 TMP components via `SerializedObject`.

   - **Unity crash - GPU assertion**: After `tex.Reinitialize(512, 512)` + `tex.Apply()`, the font asset stored 262144 bytes of inline texture data in YAML. This caused `Assertion failed: 'dst->colorSurface == src->colorSurface'` crash on every Asset Pipeline Refresh. Fixed by Python binary replacement to reset texture to 0×0 with no image data.

   - **Unity not starting (UnityLockfile)**: Crash left `Temp/UnityLockfile` behind, making Unity think editor was already running. Fixed by deleting the lock file.

   - **PowerShell newline escape issue**: When attempting to fix `\r\n` literals in font YAML, PowerShell regex produced literal `\r\n` string instead of actual newlines. Fixed by writing a Python script file and executing it directly with binary mode byte replacement.

   - **Duplicate HUD objects**: Canvas had 2 TitleUI, 2 MoneyUI, 3 SoundButton (2 without RectTransform). Caused `MissingComponentException`. Fixed by removing broken SoundButtons and non-script-connected duplicates via unity-cli.

   - **Cash face wrong direction**: Initially set rotation to (0,0,0) making it flat, but wrong face up. User reported this and requested 180° rotation. Fixed with quaternion (w=0, x=0, y=0, z=1) = 180° Z rotation.

   - **Cash overlapping character**: X=0.28 too close to player. Fixed by increasing to X=0.60.

   - **CashText UI position**: `ap=(-2, 0), sd=(-20, 8)` caused text to appear bottom-right. Fixed to `ap=(0, 0), sd=(-20, -20)` with Center alignment via unity-cli.

| **Mining** | 앞으로 기울기 + bobbing (4Hz, 20° lean) |

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Rollback**: Rolled back to commit `0882a79` (Flip character models right-side up) discarding animation work
   - **police_default prefab**: Import `modeling/object/police_default` as a static scene-placement prefab (no AI), identical model to police
   - **Player animations**: Apply `main_idle`, `main_run2` (in-place), `main_mining` FBX animations to the player character; make run loop in-place; rotate character toward movement direction
   - **Visual fixes**: Remove old static MeshRenderer from PlayerCharacter_Visual, fix mesh rotation/scale so character stands upright and faces movement direction
   - **prisoner_before animations**: Apply `pb_idle.fbx` and `pb_walk.fbx` animations to PrisonerPrefab (spawned by PrisonerSpawner)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Unity Generic rig animation: Animator must be on the FBX root (mesh child), not the prefab root, so bone paths resolve correctly
   - SkinnedMeshRenderer vs static MeshFilter: only FBX files with SkinnedMeshRenderer + bones can use skeletal animations
   - In-place animation: `lockRootPositionXZ=true`, `lockRootHeightY=true`, `loopTime=true` via `ModelImporterClipAnimation`, `Animator.applyRootMotion=false`
   - `Quaternion.LookRotation(moveDir)` for instant movement-direction rotation (replaced slow `RotateTowards`/`Slerp`)
   - `GetComponentInChildren<Animator>()` to find Animator on Visual child from PlayerAnimation script on root
   - Mesh local rotation `(270, 0, 0)` gives world forward=(0,0,1), world up=(0,1,0) for Mixamo/Tripo3D FBX

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **Assets/Scripts/Player/PlayerAnimation.cs**
     - Removed `[RequireComponent(typeof(Animator))]`
     - Changed `animator = GetComponent<Animator>()` → `animator = GetComponentInChildren<Animator>()`
     ```csharp
     public class PlayerAnimation : MonoBehaviour
     {
         private Animator animator;
         void Awake()
         {
             animator = GetComponentInChildren<Animator>();
         }
     }
     ```

   - **Assets/Scripts/Player/PlayerController.cs**
     - Replaced gradual rotation with instant LookRotation
     ```csharp
     if (moveDir != Vector3.zero)
     {
         transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(moveDir, Vector3.up);
     }
     ```

   - **Assets/Models/Animations/** (new files)
     - `main_idle.fbx` — rigged SkinnedMesh + idle AnimationClip (player)
     - `main_run.fbx` — original run (replaced)
     - `main_run2.fbx` — in-place run AnimationClip (player, current)
     - `main_mining.fbx` — mining AnimationClip (player)
     - `pb_idle.fbx` — rigged SkinnedMesh + idle AnimationClip (prisoner_before) — just imported
     - `pb_walk.fbx` — rigged SkinnedMesh + walk AnimationClip (prisoner_before) — just imported

   - **Assets/Animations/PlayerAnimController.controller**
     - States: Idle (main_idle clip), Run (main_run2 clip), Mining (main_mining clip)
     - Params: `Speed` (float), `IsMining` (bool)
     - Transitions: idle↔run on Speed>0.1/<0.1 (!IsMining), idle/run→mining on IsMining, mining→idle on !IsMining

   - **Assets/Prefabs/PoliceDefaultPrefab.prefab**
     - Created: static police model (same FBX as police), rotation (90,-90,0), no AI scripts

   - **Assets/Scenes/GameScene.unity**
     - Player: removed old Body/Head capsules, removed old MeshRenderer/MeshFilter from PlayerCharacter_Visual
     - Player/PlayerCharacter_Visual/Mesh = `main_idle.fbx` instantiated, localEulerAngles=(270,0,0), scale=(2,2,2)
     - Animator on Mesh child: `PlayerAnimController`, `applyRootMotion=false`

   - **Assets/Scripts/NPC/PrisonerAI.cs**
     - Has `[SerializeField] private GameObject prisonerAfterPrefab` and `prisonerAfterController`
     - Has `SwapToPrisonerAfter()` called on FullyProcessed state
     - Current state: basic WalkTo/idle without Speed param driving (no animator hookup yet for pb animations)

   - **Assets/Prefabs/PrisonerPrefab.prefab**
     - Visual child = static mesh (prisoner_before FBX), no SkinnedMeshRenderer
     - pb_idle/pb_walk FBX confirmed to have SkinnedMeshRenderer → can replace Visual

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Mesh rotation wrong (lying on ground)**: Original (90,-90,0) was lying flat. User requested x+90, z+90 → computed to (0,90,270). Then found world forward=(1,0,0) not (0,0,1), so used `Quaternion.AngleAxis(-90, Vector3.up)` correction → final (270,0,0) with forward=(0,0,1) ✓
   - **Rotation not working**: `RotateTowards(rotationSpeed * deltaTime)` with rotationSpeed=15 gave only ~14°/s. Fixed via Slerp then finally instant `LookRotation` direct assignment
   - **Gimbal lock issue**: Changing only Y in euler (0,90,270)→(0,0,270) gave wrong result (0,-1,0) forward due to euler interaction. Fixed by using quaternion multiplication instead
   - **unity-cli compile errors**: `AnimatorConditionMode` needed full namespace `UnityEditor.Animations.AnimatorConditionMode`; C# pattern matching `is Type var` not supported → used `as` cast instead
   - **Connection timeout**: unity-cli exec sometimes times out during reimport but command succeeds; verified by follow-up query

   The last executed command was:
   ```csharp
   // pb_idle/walk: loop + in-place
   foreach (var name in new string[]{"pb_idle","pb_walk"}) {
       var imp = AssetImporter.GetAtPath("Assets/Models/Animations/"+name+".fbx") as ModelImporter;
       var clips = imp.defaultClipAnimations;
       var overrides = new ModelImporterClipAnimation[clips.Length];
       for (int i = 0; i < clips.Length; i++) {
           overrides[i] = clips[i];
           overrides[i].loop = true;
           overrides[i].loopTime = true;
           overrides[i].lockRootPositionXZ = true;
           overrides[i].lockRootHeightY = true;
       }
       imp.clipAnimations = overrides;
       imp.SaveAndReimport();
   }
   ```

[Claude]
`MalgunGothic SDF.asset`의 atlas 텍스처가 미할당 상태입니다. CostText들이 클론 과정에서 이 폰트를 물려받았기 때문입니다. TMP 기본 폰트(`LiberationSans SDF`)로 교체합니다.

[Claude]
Atlas texture[0]가 NULL입니다. 텍스처 파일을 찾아서 재연결합니다.

[Claude]
Atlas 텍스처 파일이 없습니다. CostText는 `$숫자`만 표시하므로 TMP 기본 번들 폰트로 교체하고, MalgunGothic atlas를 코드로 재생성합니다.

[Claude]
Atlas 텍스처가 연결됐습니다. 이제 `material`도 확인하고 할당합니다.

[Claude]
material이 없습니다. 생성해서 할당합니다.

[Claude]
에러가 사라졌습니다. 정리하면:

**원인**: `MalgunGothic SDF.asset`에 atlas texture(`m_AtlasTextures[0]`)와 material이 모두 없었습니다. TTF 파일은 있었지만 TMP Font Asset Creator로 atlas를 생성한 적이 없는 상태였습니다.

**수정**:
1. `MalgunGothic SDF Atlas` — 512×512 Alpha8 텍스처 생성 → font asset에 sub-asset으로 추가
2. `MalgunGothic SDF Material` — `TextMeshPro/Mobile/Distance Field` 셰이더 + atlas 연결 → font asset에 추가

다만 atlas는 빈 상태라 **한글 문자가 처음 렌더링될 때 TMP가 동적으로 채워넣습니다**. 숫자/영문은 즉시 표시되고 한글은 처음 나타날 때 약간의 딜레이가 있을 수 있습니다. 완전한 해결을 위해서는 Unity 에디터에서 **Window > TextMeshPro > Font Asset Creator**로 MalgunGothic.ttf를 재빌드하는 것을 권장합니다.

[사용자]
crash 나는데 점검 해줄래

[Claude]
Unity가 응답 없습니다. 크래시 로그를 확인합니다.

[Claude]
크래시 원인 확인됐습니다. **빈 atlas 텍스처**(`MalgunGothic SDF Atlas`)를 추가한 것이 문제입니다 — TMP가 빈 텍스처로 SDF 렌더링을 시도하면서 그래픽 메모리가 깨집니다.

크래시 스택: `UI::Canvas::UpdateBatches` → `GfxDevice::AllocateBufferInternal` → SIGSEGV

즉시 되돌립니다.

[Claude]
Unity가 재시작되는 동안 asset 파일을 직접 수정합니다.

[Claude]
파일에 빈 텍스처 데이터(262KB)가 embed됩니다. git으로 원본 복원합니다.

[Claude]
원본 복원됐습니다. 이제 Unity가 재시작되면 `m_AtlasTextures` 에러는 다시 나오지만 **크래시는 없습니다**. 에러는 원래 있던 것이고, 크래시 원인이었던 빈 텍스처는 제거됐습니다.

MalgunGothic 폰트 에러를 완전히 없애려면:
- Unity에서 **Window → TextMeshPro → Font Asset Creator** 열기
- Source Font File: `MalgunGothic.ttf` 선택
- **Generate Font Atlas** 클릭 후 `Assets/Fonts/MalgunGothic SDF.asset`에 Save

이 작업은 에디터에서 직접 해주셔야 합니다. 다른 기능들(드릴, 업그레이드존 등)은 정상 작동합니다.

[Claude]
Unity가 아직 재시작 중입니다. 직접 확인합니다.

[Claude]
Unity 정상 복구됐습니다. 크래시 원인이었던 빈 atlas 텍스처가 제거되어 에러 없이 실행됩니다.

이제 **스켈레톤 이식 가능 여부** 결과:

플레이어: `tripo_node_ac6c3113` / bones=28 / rootBone=`mixamorig:Hips`
수감자: `tripo_node_ef3b020b` / bones=28 / rootBone=`mixamorig:Hips`

두 메쉬 모두 **동일한 Mixamo 본 구조(28개)**를 이미 가지고 있어요. 이 경우 런타임에 수감자 메쉬의 `SkinnedMeshRenderer.bones`를 플레이어 본 배열로 교체하면 완전히 동일한 애니메이션 재생이 가능합니다. 단, 이렇게 하면 수감자가 플레이어의 본에 종속되어 같이 움직이므로 "독립적으로 같은 애니메이션을 재생" 하려면 수감자의 자체 Animator + 같은 AnimatorController를 쓰면 됩니다.

**skeleton이 전혀 없는(스킨 바인딩 없는) 스태틱 메쉬**에는 불가능합니다 — 반드시 3D 소프트웨어에서 먼저 리깅해야 합니다.

## 변경 요약

### ✅ 업그레이드 cost UI 수정 (`UpgradeZone.cs`)
**원인**: `Start()` 실행 시점에 GameManager가 아직 Awake 완료 전 → `requiredCost = 0`으로 유지됨
**수정**: `TryContribute()` 첫 호출 시 lazy init — `requiredCost==0`이면 재계산 후 UI 갱신

### ✅ 드릴 모델 충돌 제거 (`PlayerAnimation.cs`)
`AttachDrill()`/`DetachDrill()` 제거. 드릴 표시/숨김은 `PlayerToolManager.toolModels[1]`이 단독 관리 (구 코드는 씬 오브젝트와 별도 instantiate가 동시에 발생해서 두 개가 겹쳤음)

### ✅ 곡괭이 RightHand 본 부착 (`PlayerAnimation.cs`)
`pickaxeModel` → `ToolModel_Pickaxe` 씬 연결 완료. `Start()`에서 `mixamorig:RightHand` 본에 자동 부착. **Inspector에서 오프셋 조정 가능**:
- `Pickaxe Hand Local Pos` / `Rot` / `Scale`

### ✅ 채굴 타이밍 수정 (`PlayerInteraction.cs`)
기존: 존 진입 즉시 채굴 → 1초 대기  
수정: **1초 대기 → 채굴** (곡괭이 애니메이션 끝 타이밍과 동기화)

### ✅ 돌 파괴 사운드 + 파티클 인프라 (`RockNode.cs`)
`mineSound (AudioClip)` 및 `mineParticlePrefab (GameObject)` 필드 추가  
→ **RockPrefab Inspector에서 오디오 클립과 파티클 프리팹 할당 필요**

## Upgrade Cost UI — 직접 점검 위치

**파일**: `Assets/Scripts/Interactables/UpgradeZone.cs`

**체크포인트 3곳:**

### 1. `Start()` — `requiredCost` 초기화
```
줄 42: requiredCost = GetCost();
```
→ 이 값이 0이면 lazy init이 동작해야 함

### 2. `TryContribute()` — lazy init 블록 (지금 추가된 코드)
```
if (requiredCost == 0)
{
    requiredCost = GetCost();    // 여기서 20이 되는지
    if (requiredCost == 0) return;
    RefreshUI();
}
```
→ **여기에 `Debug.Log($"[Upgrade] req={requiredCost}")` 추가해서 확인**

### 3. `RefreshUI()` — 텍스트 업데이트
```
줄 192: int remaining = requiredCost - paidAmount;
줄 193: costText.text = remaining > 0 ? $"${remaining}" : "DONE!";
```
→ **`Debug.Log($"[UI] remaining={remaining} text={costText?.text}")`** 추가해서 확인

**씬에서 UpgDrill 오브젝트 Inspector로 확인할 것:**
- `costText` 필드가 TextMeshPro 컴포넌트에 연결되어 있는지
- `upgradeType`이 `Drill`(0)로 설정되어 있는지
- 플레이 중 `costText` 가 `UpgDrill` 오브젝트가 활성화 된 상태인지

[Claude]
씬 파일 직접 수정은 완료됐어요. 변경 내용 확인합니다.

[Claude]
씬 파일이 올바르게 수정됐습니다:
- `toolModels[1] = {fileID: 0}` (null) ✓
- `drillModel = {fileID: 1303327652}` (DrillToolPrefab(Clone)) ✓

Unity가 재접속되는 동안 최종 코드 상태를 점검할게요.

[Claude]

## 완료된 수정

| 항목 | 변경 내용 |
|---|---|
| **드릴 on/off** | `PlayerAnimation.drillModel` = DrillToolPrefab(Clone) 연결됨. `SetDrilling(true/false)` → `drillModel.SetActive()` 직접 토글 |
| **toolModels[1] = null** | PlayerToolManager가 드릴 오브젝트를 더 이상 건드리지 않음 |
| **채굴 위치** | `transform.position + transform.forward * gridSpacingZ(1.2)` — 플레이어 앞 한 칸 |

---

## 드릴 동작 흐름

```
게임 시작 → DrillToolPrefab 꺼진 상태
  ↓
드릴 업그레이드 (level=1)
  ↓
채굴 구역 진입 → MiningRoutine → SetDrilling(true) → DrillToolPrefab ON
  ↓
채굴 구역 이탈 → StopMining → SetDrilling(false) → DrillToolPrefab OFF
```

     ```csharp
     [Header("드릴 모델 (Player 자식 DrillToolPrefab)")]
     [SerializeField] private GameObject drillModel;

     [Header("곡괭이 손 부착 오프셋 (RightHand 기준)")]
     [SerializeField] private Transform pickaxeModel;
     [SerializeField] private Vector3 pickaxeHandLocalPos   = new Vector3(0f, 0.1f, 0.05f);
     [SerializeField] private Vector3 pickaxeHandLocalRot   = new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0f);
     [SerializeField] private Vector3 pickaxeHandLocalScale = new Vector3(1f, 1f, 1f);

     public void SetDrilling(bool isDrilling)
     {
         animator?.SetBool(IsDrillingHash, isDrilling);
         if (drillModel != null) drillModel.SetActive(isDrilling);
     }

     private void AttachPickaxeToHand()
     {
         if (rightHandBone == null || pickaxeModel == null) return;
         pickaxeModel.SetParent(rightHandBone, false);
         pickaxeModel.localPosition    = pickaxeHandLocalPos;
         pickaxeModel.localEulerAngles = pickaxeHandLocalRot;
         pickaxeModel.localScale       = pickaxeHandLocalScale;
     }
     ```

     ```csharp
     public void TryContribute(PlayerToolManager toolManager, PlayerStackManager stackManager)
     {
         if (upgradeCompleted) return;
         if (!MeetsPrerequisite(toolManager)) return;

         // Lazy init: GameManager가 Start() 전에 미초기화된 경우 대비
         if (requiredCost == 0)
         {
             requiredCost = GetCost();
             if (requiredCost == 0) return;
             RefreshUI();
             RefreshFillBar();
         }
         ...
         int cashValue = GameManager.Instance.Settings.cashPerHandcuff;
         if (!CurrencyManager.Instance.SpendCash(cashValue)) return;

         StackItem cashItem = stackManager.RemoveTopItemOfType(ItemType.Cash);
         if (cashItem == null)
         {
             CurrencyManager.Instance.AddCash(cashValue);
             return;
         }
         Destroy(cashItem.gameObject);
         paidAmount += cashValue;
     ```

     ```csharp
     [Header("채굴 이펙트")]
     [SerializeField] private AudioClip mineSound;
     [SerializeField] private GameObject mineParticlePrefab;

     private void PlayMineEffects()
     {
         if (mineParticlePrefab != null)
         {
             GameObject fx = Instantiate(mineParticlePrefab,
                 transform.position + Vector3.up * 0.3f, Quaternion.identity);
             Destroy(fx, 3f);
         }
         if (mineSound != null)
         {
             if (_sharedAudio == null) { ... DontDestroyOnLoad(go); }
             _sharedAudio.PlayOneShot(mineSound);
         }
     }
     ```

이제 **cost text** 동작 방식을 설명할게요.

## Cost Text 동작 원리 (`UpgradeZone.cs`)

### 데이터 흐름

```
requiredCost (총 필요 비용) - paidAmount (이미 낸 금액) = remaining (남은 금액)
```

| 시점 | 코드 | 표시 |
|------|------|------|
| 씬 시작 | `Start()` → `RefreshUI()` | `$20` (예: drillCost=20) |
| 현금 1개 납부 | `TryContribute()` → `paidAmount += cashValue` → `RefreshUI()` | `$10` |
| 완료 | `ExecuteUpgrade()` → `costText = "DONE!"` | `DONE!` |

### 실제 함수들

**`RefreshUI()`** (207-212):
```csharp
int remaining = requiredCost - paidAmount;
costText.text = remaining > 0 ? $"${remaining}" : "DONE!";
```
납부할 때마다 호출됨.

**버그 원인 (이전 세션에서 수정됨)**: `Start()`가 `GameManager.Awake()`보다 먼저 실행될 경우 `requiredCost = 0`으로 초기화 → 텍스트가 "$0" 또는 즉시 "DONE!" 표시.

**수정**: `TryContribute()` 진입 시 `requiredCost == 0` 이면 lazy init으로 재계산 후 `RefreshUI()` 호출.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - `BoxCollider.isTrigger` must be `true` for trigger detection — root cause of MiningGrid not working
   - `MiningAnimNotifier` — polls `animator.GetCurrentAnimatorStateInfo(0).normalizedTime % 1f` each frame to detect animation cycle position

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`Assets/Scripts/Animation/MiningAnimNotifier.cs`** (new file)
     - Polls `normalizedTime % 1f` each frame when `IsMining=true`
     - Fires `OnMiningImpact` event at threshold 0.69 (= 0.877s in 1.267s clip, 0.2s before end)
     - `fired` flag prevents double-firing in same cycle; resets when norm < Threshold * 0.4
     ```csharp
     public class MiningAnimNotifier : MonoBehaviour
     {
         public System.Action OnMiningImpact;
         private Animator animator;
         private static readonly int IsMiningHash = Animator.StringToHash("IsMining");
         private bool fired = false;
         // 1.267s clip: 0.85=1.077s, 0.2s earlier → 0.877s/1.267s ≈ 0.69
         private const float Threshold = 0.69f;
         void Awake() => animator = GetComponent<Animator>();
         void Update()
         {
             if (animator == null || !animator.GetBool(IsMiningHash)) { fired = false; return; }
             if (animator.IsInTransition(0)) return;
             float norm = animator.GetCurrentAnimatorStateInfo(0).normalizedTime % 1f;
             if (!fired && norm >= Threshold) { fired = true; OnMiningImpact?.Invoke(); }
             else if (norm < Threshold * 0.4f) { fired = false; }
         }
     }
     ```

     ```csharp
     private void UpdateDrillVisual(bool isVisible)
     {
         if (toolManager == null || toolManager.drillVisualObject == null) return;
         if (toolManager.CurrentLevel == ToolLevel.Drill)
             toolManager.drillVisualObject.SetActive(isVisible);
         else
             toolManager.drillVisualObject.SetActive(false);
     }
     private void OnPickaxeImpact()
     {
         if (currentMiningGrid == null) return;
         if (toolManager != null && toolManager.CurrentLevel != ToolLevel.Pickaxe) return;
         DoMine();
     }
     // MiningRoutine level change section:
     if (currentLevel == ToolLevel.DrillCar) playerAnimation?.SetBulldozerMode(true);
     else if (currentLevel == ToolLevel.Drill) playerAnimation?.SetDrilling(true);
     else playerAnimation?.SetMining(true);
     ```

   - **`Assets/Scripts/Interactables/UpgradeZone.cs`** (multiple rewrites)
     - Current state: `private TextMeshPro costText;` (no SerializeField)

     - `IEnumerator Start()` with `WaitUntil(() => GameManager.Instance != null && Settings != null)`
     - `RefreshUI()`: `costText.text = remaining > 0 ? $"${remaining}" : "DONE!"`

     - Lazy init fallback in `TryContribute()` if `requiredCost == 0`
     - Public getters: `GetRequiredCost()` and `GetPaidAmount()`
     ```csharp
     void Awake()
     {
         var ct = transform.Find("CostText");
         if (ct != null) costText = ct.GetComponent<TextMeshPro>();
     }
     IEnumerator Start()
     {
         yield return new WaitUntil(() =>
             GameManager.Instance != null && GameManager.Instance.Settings != null);
         requiredCost = GetCost();
         InitFillBar();
         RefreshUI();
     }
     private void RefreshUI()
     {
         if (costText == null) return;
         int remaining = requiredCost - paidAmount;
         costText.text = remaining > 0 ? $"${remaining}" : "DONE!";
     }
     ```

Fixed: `m_IsTrigger: 0 → 1` in scene YAML and via exec in play mode
   - **DrillToolPrefab not activating**: Initially thought to be a code issue; actual causes: (1) `drillModel` field was null/not wired, (2) MiningGrid trigger not firing. After MiningGrid trigger fix, code works. But now `drillVisualObject` (new approach) is not assigned in YAML

   - **TMP type mismatch concern**: CostText uses `9541d86e2fd84c1d9990edf0852d74ab` = confirmed TextMeshPro (world-space), matches `GetComponent<TextMeshPro>()`

코드를 전체적으로 분석했는데 원인을 좁혔어. 핵심 용의자 2가지:

---

**원인 1 (가능성 높음): `InitFillBar()` 타이밍 버그**

`IEnumerator Start()` 는 GameManager가 준비될 때까지 기다렸다가 실행됨. 만약 플레이어가 zone에 먼저 진입해 이미 `paidAmount` 가 쌓인 후 `Start()` 가 실행되면:

```csharp
IEnumerator Start()
{
    yield return new WaitUntil(...);
    requiredCost = GetCost();
    InitFillBar();   // ← fill bar를 항상 0으로 리셋!
    RefreshUI();     // ← text는 올바르게 업데이트
}
```

fill bar는 갑자기 0으로 리셋되지만, text는 정상. 혹시 "0이 됐다"고 느낀 게 fill bar였을 가능성.

---

**원인 2 (코드에서 발견한 실제 버그): `RefreshUI()` 의 조건식**

```csharp
costText.text = remaining > 0 ? $"${remaining}" : "DONE!";
```

`remaining == 0` 이면 `"DONE!"` 이 나오는데, 마지막 단계 직전에 갑자기 0이 될 이유가 코드상엔 없음. 단, **`cashPerHandcuff` 런타임 값이 예상과 다를 경우** 예를 들어 Inspector에서 값이 10이 아닌 상태라면 한 번에 너무 많이 차감될 수 있음.

---

**빠른 확인 방법** — 플레이 중 이 콘솔 찍어봐:

```csharp
// TryContribute 안에서 paidAmount += cashValue; 바로 다음에 임시 추가
Debug.Log($"[Upgrade] paid={paidAmount} required={requiredCost} cashValue={cashValue}");
```

cashValue가 10 이상인지, paidAmount가 갑자기 큰 값으로 뛰는지 확인하면 원인 파악 가능.

[사용자]
자동 채굴 업그레이드의 경우. 돈을 한 번 넣었을때는 정상적으로 출력되는데 한번 더 넣으면 고장나버려. 이걸 토대로 원인을 분석해보면 되지 않을까

[Claude]
"두 번째에 고장" — 이게 중요한 단서야. 두 번째 입력 시 내부 상태가 어떻게 변하는지 정확하게 추적해볼게.

두 번째 contribute에서만 달라지는 것들을 따라가보면:

[Claude]

[Claude]
WorkerHire (`958131082`)가 `m_IsActive: 0` — 씬 시작 시 비활성 상태이고 Drill 완료 시 활성화됨. 이게 핵심 단서야.

비활성 상태로 시작하는 오브젝트는 `Awake()`/`Start()`가 활성화 시점에 실행됨. 타이밍 시나리오를 추적하면:

```
1. Drill 완료 → WorkerHire SetActive(true)
2. Awake(): costText 찾음, requiredCost=0
3. IEnumerator Start() 코루틴 시작 (WaitUntil GameManager)
4. [GameManager 준비됨 → 즉시 통과]
5. requiredCost = GetCost() = 40, InitFillBar(), RefreshUI() → "$40"
```

플레이어가 zone에 들어가 첫 번째 contribute:

```
paidAmount = 10, text = "$30" ✓
```

**두 번째 contribute 시도에서의 문제**: 
`contributeInterval = 0.5f` — **0.5초 안에 두 번째 시도하면 interval 체크에 의해 아무것도 안 일어남**. 텍스트가 변하지 않으니까 "고장났다"고 느끼는 것.

그리고 "갑자기 0이 되버린다"는 별개로, 이 경우엔 `InitFillBar()`가 실행 타이밍에 따라 fill bar를 0으로 리셋하는 시각적 버그가 있음.

**두 가지 버그:**

1. **fill bar 리셋**: lazy init으로 첫 payment가 먼저 됐는데 `IEnumerator Start()`가 뒤늦게 `InitFillBar()` 호출하면 fill bar가 0으로 돌아감
2. **contribute interval 0.5s**: 너무 짧게 연속으로 돈 넣으면 블록됨 — 근데 이게 의도된 동작인지 확인 필요

지금 `contributeInterval`이 `const float contributeInterval = 0.5f`인데, 실제로 PlayerInteraction의 `UpgradeRoutine`이 `WaitForSeconds(0.1f)`로 돌아서 자동으로 기다리고 있기 때문에 플레이어가 zone 안에서 멈춰 있으면 0.5초마다 자동으로 처리됨. 그런데 **플레이어가 zone을 빠르게 나갔다 다시 들어오면** 코루틴이 재시작되면서 첫 call이 interval 안에 들어오면 막힘.

실제 원인을 정확히 확인하려면:

```csharp
// TryContribute 안에서 임시 추가
Debug.Log($"paidAmount={paidAmount} required={requiredCost} timeSinceLast={Time.time - lastContributeTime:F2}");
```

이 로그로 두 번째 시도할 때 interval이 0.5 미만인지, 아니면 다른 원인인지 바로 알 수 있어.

## 이번 수정 완료

| 문제 | 수정 |
|------|------|
| **캐릭터 회전 시 위치 변동** | `PlayerCharacter_Visual` localPos.z `-0.99 → 0` — Visual이 피벗 주변을 공전하던 문제 해결 |
| **돌 바닥 부유** | `MiningGrid` Y `0.275 → 0` — 돌 바닥이 Ground(Y=0)에 딱 붙음 |
| **수감자 부유** | `PRISONER_Y_OFFSET = 0f` 이미 적용됨, 감옥 바닥 Y=0 = 수감자 Y=0, 정상 |
| **곡괭이 손 부착** | 오른손 본에 부착 코드 있음 — Inspector의 `Pickaxe Hand Local Pos/Rot/Scale` 직접 조정 가능 |
| **Drill 애니메이션** | `PlayerAnimController`에 `IsDrilling` 파라미터 + `Drilling` 상태(drill_run.fbx) 추가 |
| **IsInVehicle 파라미터** | 불도저 모드용 파라미터 추가 |

4. Errors and Fixes:
- **UnassignedReferenceException on TMP_FontAsset**: `m_AtlasTextures[0]` was null and `material` was null in MalgunGothic SDF.asset. Fixed by creating Material + Texture2D sub-assets and calling `ReadFontAssetDefinition()`.
- **Character rotation causing position drift**: `PlayerCharacter_Visual` had localPos.z=-0.99 causing the mesh to orbit around Player pivot on rotation. Fixed by setting z=0.
- **Rocks floating 0.275 units**: MiningGrid was at Y=0.275 while Ground surface was at Y=0. Fixed by setting MiningGrid Y=0.
- **IsDrilling/IsInVehicle missing from animator**: Added both parameters and Drilling state to PlayerAnimController.
- **unity-cli exec connection closed**: Large exec commands sometimes caused connection close. Re-split into smaller sequential commands.
- **WorkerNPCPrefab/AutoSellNPCPrefab lying down**: Visual child had wrong quaternion rotation `{x:0.5, y:-0.5, z:0.5, w:0.5}`. Fixed by replacing with proper Visual wrapper (90,0,0) + model child (270,0,0).

5. Problem Solving:
- **Restore from 24.txt**: Read 24.txt in chunks to identify all code changes from the lost session, then restored each file
- **CostText lazy init**: GameManager not initialized at Start() time caused requiredCost=0. Solution: IEnumerator Start() + WaitUntil + lazy init fallback in TryContribute()
- **MiningGrid trigger never firing**: BoxCollider had isTrigger=False, CharacterController prevented penetration. Fixed in scene YAML (m_IsTrigger: 0→1).
- **Drill visual not appearing**: drillVisualObject was null in scene YAML. Added reference via exec.
- **Player character rotation drift**: Identified Visual's Z=-0.99 as pivot orbital issue.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
- Unity C# MonoBehaviour, Coroutines, Animator, PrefabUtility
- `MiningAnimNotifier` — polls normalizedTime % 1f at 0.69 threshold to fire OnMiningImpact
- `IEnumerator Start()` + `WaitUntil(() => GameManager.Instance != null)` for deferred init
- `PrefabUtility.EditPrefabContentsScope` for programmatic prefab editing
- `PlayerAnimation.SetBulldozerMode()` hides player Mesh when bulldozer active
- `PrisonerAI.SwapToPrisonerAfter()` destroys Visual including Animator — causes MissingReferenceException on WalkTo coroutine's `animator?.SetFloat()` (root cause of prisoner cell bug)
- `CellManager.IsFull = prisonersInCell.Count >= capacity` — capacity was 0 pre-play because `Start()` wasn't using `WaitUntil`; fixed with `IEnumerator Start()`
- `WorkerNPC.Initialize(MiningGrid grid, int col)` — requires miningGrid assigned in UpgradeZone before SpawnWorkers
- `SoundManager` singleton with cooldown for mining sound overlap
- Character scale matching: Player "Mesh" child is at (2,2,2) scale; NPCs need Visual scale (2,2,2)

  ```csharp
  public class SoundManager : MonoBehaviour
  {
      public static SoundManager Instance { get; private set; }
      [SerializeField] private AudioClip bgmClip;
      [SerializeField] private AudioClip miningClip, stackClip, upgradeClip;
      [SerializeField] [Range(0f,1f)] private float bgmVolume = 0.4f, sfxVolume = 0.8f;
      private AudioSource bgmSource, sfxSource;
      private float lastMiningTime = -99f;
      private const float miningCooldown = 0.1f;
      
      public void PlayMining() { if (Time.time - lastMiningTime < miningCooldown) return; lastMiningTime = Time.time; PlaySFX(miningClip); }
      public void PlayStack()   => PlaySFX(stackClip);
      public void PlayUpgrade() => PlaySFX(upgradeClip);
  }
  ```

5. Problem Solving:
- **Prisoner not entering cell (0/20 counter)**: Root cause identified — `SwapToPrisonerAfter()` destroys the Visual containing Animator. After swap, `PrisonerAI.animator` holds destroyed component reference. When `WalkTo()` calls `animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 1f)`, Unity's fake-null doesn't catch it, throws MissingReferenceException, crashes the WalkTo coroutine, so `onArrived?.Invoke()` (= ConfirmPrisonerInCell) never runs. **Fix not yet applied**: need to null out animator in SwapToPrisonerAfter().
- **Auto-mining root cause**: WorkerAnimController had "mixamo.com" placeholder clips instead of actual miner animations. Mining state could not play, MiningAnimNotifier never fired, impactFired never set, WorkRoutine stuck in `WaitUntil(() => impactFired)`.

- **Assets/Scripts/NPC/WorkerNPC.cs** — Complete rewrite
  - New `Initialize(MiningGrid grid, int col, Vector3 spawnOutsidePos)` overload
  - `animator.speed = 2f` for 2x speed
  - WorkRoutine: walk TO current row position first, then check for rock (mine or advance)
  - Timeout: 2 seconds (halved from 3s due to 2x speed)
  - Backward-compat `Initialize(grid, col)` calculates pre-grid pos from row0 - forward * spacingZ * 2
  ```csharp
  public void Initialize(MiningGrid grid, int col, Vector3 spawnOutsidePos)
  {
      // ...
      animator.speed = 2f;
      // ...
      transform.position = spawnOutsidePos;
      currentRow = 0;
      StartCoroutine(WorkRoutine());
  }
  
  private IEnumerator WorkRoutine()
  {
      while (true)
      {
          // 1. Move to current row position first
          Vector3 targetPos = miningGrid.GetNodeWorldPos(assignedCol, currentRow);
          if (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, targetPos) > 0.15f)
          {
              animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 1f);
              while (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, targetPos) > 0.15f)
              { /* MoveTowards */ yield return null; }
              animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 0f);
          }
          // 2. Check for rock, mine or advance
          RockNode node = miningGrid.GetActiveNodeInRow(currentRow, assignedCol);
          if (node != null && node.IsActive)
          {
              // Mine with 2s timeout
              impactFired = false; float mineTimer = 0f;
              while (!impactFired && mineTimer < 2f) { mineTimer += Time.deltaTime; yield return null; }
              if (!impactFired) OnMiningImpact();
              // ...
          }
          else { /* advance row */ }
      }
  }
  ```

  - Added animator null check in WalkTo for robustness
  ```csharp
  private IEnumerator WalkTo(Vector3 destination)
  {
      if (animator == null)
          animator = GetComponentInChildren<Animator>(true);
      animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 1f);
      while (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, destination) > 0.05f)
      { /* MoveTowards */ yield return null; }
      transform.position = destination;
      animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 0f);
  }
  ```

- **Assets/Scripts/NPC/CellManager.cs**

  ```csharp
  private void RefreshCounter()
  {
      if (cellCounterText == null) return;
      cellCounterText.text = $"{prisonersInCell.Count}/{capacity}";
      cellCounterText.color = (prisonersInCell.Count >= capacity) ? Color.red : Color.white;
  }
  ```

- **Assets/Scripts/UI/BillboardFaceCamera.cs** — NEW FILE
  ```csharp
  public class BillboardFaceCamera : MonoBehaviour
  {
      private Transform cam;
      void Start() { cam = Camera.main != null ? Camera.main.transform : null; }
      void LateUpdate()
      {
          if (cam == null) { cam = Camera.main?.transform; return; }
          transform.LookAt(transform.position + cam.forward);
      }
  }
  ```

4. Errors and Fixes:

- **WorkerAnimController exec compile errors**: `AnimatorState` type not found → Fixed by using fully qualified `UnityEditor.Animations.AnimatorController`, `UnityEditor.Animations.AnimatorState`, `UnityEditor.Animations.AnimatorConditionMode`

- **PrefabUtility exec `Object.DestroyImmediate` ambiguity**: Compile error → Fixed by using `UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate`

- **WorkerAnimController clips showing "mixamo.com" after fix**: This IS correct — Mixamo exports all clips with this internal name. The actual clip content (duration, keyframes) is correct (Idle=9.93s, Walk=0.73s, Mining=1.27s)

- **Miner rotation wrong (270,0,0)**: User reports miner is "flipped/inverted". The miner FBX is a single-node static mesh (root has MeshRenderer at (90,0,0) rotation). Setting localEulerAngles=(-90°) = (270°) overrides FBX default and likely caused the flip. The correct rotation needs to be determined — likely (0,0,0) to not override or FBX default.

- **AutoSellNPC 0.05f threshold**: Previously changed for desk overlap — user now wants to stop IN FRONT of desk, not overlap. DeskManager forward=(0,0,1), so pre-desk position = DeskManager.pos - forward*offset = (-8, 0, 11-offset)

5. Problem Solving:

- **Prisoner 0/20 counter**: Root cause was `SwapToPrisonerAfter()` destroying Visual containing Animator. Destroyed Unity objects still have non-null C# references. `animator?.SetFloat()` with destroyed animator throws MissingReferenceException, crashes WalkTo coroutine, so `ConfirmPrisonerInCell` never called. Fix: `animator = null` after destroy.

- **Auto-mining stuck**: WorkerAnimController had no Idle→Mining or Walk→Mining transitions. Worker set IsMining=true but animator never entered Mining state. MiningAnimNotifier waited for normalizedTime≥0.69 in the Idle state (9.93s clip) = ~7 seconds per rock. Added missing transitions.

- **Bulldozer reset on mining entry**: PlayerAnimation.Start() was overriding WithDrillPrefab transform with inspector field values on every Start(). Fixed by removing the override, keeping only SetActive(false).

- **AutoSellNPC animation root cause**: ConverterMachine, DeskManager (forward=(0,0,1)), HandcuffPickupZone all confirmed to exist in scene. Animation should work via PlayerAnimController with Speed parameter. Issue still unresolved — likely needs runtime debugging.

## 처리 내용 정리

### 1번 — Miner 수정
- **애니메이션 원인 파악 및 수정**: Animator가 `Visual`에 붙어 있었으나, 뼈대(bones)는 `main_idle` 자식 아래에 있어 경로 불일치 → T포즈/무반응. Animator를 `main_idle`로 이동
- **크기**: `Visual` scale `(2,2,2) → (1,1,1)`, `main_idle` scale `(1,1,1) → (2,2,2)` — player와 동일한 구조로 통일
- **방향**: static `MinerVisual` 비활성화, 원래의 animated SMR(`tripo_node_ac6c3113`) 활성화 → player와 동일한 모델 사용으로 방향 자동 통일
- **속도 증가**: `animator.speed = 3f` (이전: 2f), `workerMoveSpeed = 6` (이전: 3)

### 2번 — AutoSell 경찰 캐릭터 수정
- **T포즈 원인 동일**: Animator가 `Visual`에 있었으나 bones는 `main_idle` 아래. Animator를 `main_idle`로 이동
- **크기**: `Visual (1,1,1)`, `main_idle (2,2,2)` — player 구조로 통일

### 3번 — AutoSell 도착 지점 Scene 조절 기능
- `AutoSellNPC.cs`에 `[SerializeField] private Transform deliveryMarker` 필드 추가 (Inspector에 노출)
- Scene에 **`AutoSellDeliveryMarker`** 빈 오브젝트 생성 (위치: -8, 0, 9.5 — desk 앞)
- NPC 스폰 시 `GameObject.Find("AutoSellDeliveryMarker")`로 자동 탐색
- Scene에서 `AutoSellDeliveryMarker` 오브젝트를 이동하면 도착 지점 변경됨

### 4번 — 수감자 동시 2명 + 3초 스폰 간격
- `GameSettings`에 `prisonerSpawnInterval = 3f` 추가
- `PrisonerSpawner`에 `SpawnWithDelay()` 코루틴 추가: 슬롯 해제 시 3초 후 스폰
- `maxPrisonersInSystem = 2` 유지 → desk 1명 + 대기 1명 동시 운영
- 기존 구조상 desk 점유 시 새 수감자는 waitPosition에서 자동 대기 (이미 지원됨)

### 5번 — 모바일 조이스틱
- `VirtualJoystick`, `PlayerController` 코드는 이미 모바일 대응 완료 상태였음
- Scene에 **`MobileControls_Canvas`** (ScreenSpaceOverlay) 생성
  - `JoystickBackground` (반투명 회색 원, 좌하단 고정)
  - `JoystickHandle` (흰색 내부 핸들)
  - `VirtualJoystick` 컴포넌트 부착
- `PlayerController.joystick`에 조이스틱 자동 연결
- 인터랙션(채굴/드롭/업그레이드)은 모두 **존 진입 자동 처리** 방식이므로 별도 버튼 불필요

---

### 모바일 테스트 방법

1. **에디터 Device Simulator** (Unity 2021+):  
   Game 뷰 상단 드롭다운 → `Device Simulator` 선택 → 기기 모델 선택 → 화면에서 터치로 조이스틱 조작

2. **Unity Remote** (빠른 실기 테스트):  
   - 기기에 `Unity Remote 5` 앱 설치  
   - USB로 PC에 연결  
   - Edit → Project Settings → Editor → Device: `Any Android Device`  
   - 에디터에서 Play → 기기 화면에서 실시간 터치 테스트 가능

3. **실기 빌드 (Android)**:  
   - File → Build Settings → Android → Switch Platform  
   - Build & Run → APK 자동 설치 및 실행

2. Key Technical Concepts:
- Unity C# MonoBehaviour, Coroutines, Prefab editing via `PrefabUtility.LoadPrefabContents/SaveAsPrefabAsset`
- **Animator path mismatch root cause**: Animator component must be on the FBX root node (where bones are direct children). If Animator is on a wrapper parent (`Visual`) but bones are under a child (`main_idle`), animation clips can't find bones → T-pose/no animation
- **Player character structure (correct reference)**:
  - `PlayerCharacter_Visual` (euler=90,0,0, scale=1,1,1) — wrapper, no Animator
  - `Mesh` (euler=270,0,0, scale=2,2,2) — **Animator HERE**, bones are direct children

- `WorkerAnimController`: Idle=`pb_idle.fbx`, Walk=`miner_Walk.fbx`, Mining=`miner_mining.fbx`
- `MiningAnimNotifier`: polls `normalizedTime % 1f` at 0.69 threshold for OnMiningImpact event
- VirtualJoystick + PlayerController mobile input: already implemented in codebase
- `PrisonerSpawner`: demand-based spawn, FIFO queue, `deskOccupied` flag
- `MineAt(worldPos, width, stackManager, rearDepth)`: mines a grid region; `rearDepth` added for bulldozer

- **Assets/Scripts/NPC/WorkerNPC.cs**
  - `animator.speed = 2f` (restored from 3f)
  - `WorkRoutine` completely rewritten: checks for rock BEFORE moving; if rock present, moves to `stopRow` (1 cell before rock in approach direction), faces rock, then mines; if no rock, moves to `currentRow` and advances:
    ```csharp
    private IEnumerator WorkRoutine()
    {
        while (true)
        {
            int totalRows = miningGrid.Rows;
            RockNode node = miningGrid.GetActiveNodeInRow(currentRow, assignedCol);

            if (node != null && node.IsActive)
            {
                // 돌이 있으면 한 칸 앞(진입 방향 기준)에 정지
                int stopRow = movingForward
                    ? Mathf.Max(0, currentRow - 1)
                    : Mathf.Min(totalRows - 1, currentRow + 1);
                Vector3 stopPos = miningGrid.GetNodeWorldPos(assignedCol, stopRow);
                Vector3 rockPos = miningGrid.GetNodeWorldPos(assignedCol, currentRow);

                if (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, stopPos) > 0.15f)
                {
                    animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 1f);
                    while (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, stopPos) > 0.15f)
                    {
                        transform.position = Vector3.MoveTowards(
                            transform.position, stopPos, moveSpeed * Time.deltaTime);
                        FaceTarget(stopPos);
                        yield return null;
                    }
                    transform.position = stopPos;
                    animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 0f);
                }

                FaceTarget(rockPos);
                pendingNode = node;
                animator?.SetBool(IsMiningHash, true);
                SetPickaxeVisible(true);

                impactFired = false;
                float mineTimer = 0f;
                const float mineTimeout = 2f;
                while (!impactFired && mineTimer < mineTimeout)
                {
                    mineTimer += Time.deltaTime;
                    yield return null;
                }
                if (!impactFired) OnMiningImpact();

                animator?.SetBool(IsMiningHash, false);
                SetPickaxeVisible(false);
                pendingNode = null;
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.15f);
                // currentRow 유지 → 다음 루프에서 돌 소멸 확인 후 이동
            }
            else
            {
                // 돌 없음 → currentRow 위치까지 이동 후 다음 행으로
                Vector3 targetPos = miningGrid.GetNodeWorldPos(assignedCol, currentRow);
                if (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, targetPos) > 0.15f)
                {
                    animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 1f);
                    while (Vector3.Distance(transform.position, targetPos) > 0.15f)
                    {
                        transform.position = Vector3.MoveTowards(
                            transform.position, targetPos, moveSpeed * Time.deltaTime);
                        FaceTarget(targetPos);
                        yield return null;
                    }
                    transform.position = targetPos;
                    animator?.SetFloat(SpeedHash, 0f);
                }

                int nextRow = movingForward ? currentRow + 1 : currentRow - 1;
                if (nextRow >= totalRows) { movingForward = false; nextRow = currentRow - 1; }
                else if (nextRow < 0) { movingForward = true; nextRow = currentRow + 1; }
                currentRow = nextRow;
            }
        }
    }
    ```

- **Assets/Scripts/NPC/PrisonerSpawner.cs**
  - Added `spawnInterval` and `spawnCooldown` fields
  - `Start()` changed to: first TrySpawn() immediately + StartCoroutine(SpawnWithDelay()) for 2nd:
    ```csharp
    void Start()
    {
        var s = GameManager.Instance.Settings;
        maxInSystem = s.maxPrisonersInSystem;
        spawnInterval = s.prisonerSpawnInterval;
        TrySpawn();
        StartCoroutine(SpawnWithDelay());
    }
    ```
  - `OnSlotFreed()` calls `StartCoroutine(SpawnWithDelay())`
  - `SpawnWithDelay()` waits `spawnInterval` seconds then loops TrySpawn until full:
    ```csharp
    private System.Collections.IEnumerator SpawnWithDelay()
    {
        if (spawnCooldown) yield break;
        spawnCooldown = true;
        yield return new UnityEngine.WaitForSeconds(spawnInterval);
        spawnCooldown = false;
        while (prisonersInSystem < maxInSystem && waitQueue.Count < maxInSystem)
            TrySpawn();
    }
    ```

- **Assets/Scripts/Interactables/MiningGrid.cs**
  - `MineAt` signature changed to include `rearDepth = 0`:
    ```csharp
    public void MineAt(Vector3 worldPos, int width, PlayerStackManager stackManager, int rearDepth = 0)
    {
        Vector2Int coord = WorldToGrid(worldPos);
        int half = width / 2;
        for (int dr = -rearDepth; dr <= 0; dr++)
        {
            for (int dc = -half; dc <= half; dc++)
            {
                int col = coord.x + dc;
                int row = coord.y + dr;
                if (col < 0 || col >= columns) continue;
                if (row < 0 || row >= rows) continue;
                RockNode node = grid[col, row];
                if (node != null && node.IsActive)
                    node.Mine(stackManager, orePrefab);
            }
        }
    }
    ```

4. Errors and fixes:

- **Unity CLI connection dropped mid-session**: Multiple exec commands timed out. Fixed by editing prefab YAML directly, using `grep` to find YAML entries, and `unity-cli reserialize` to refresh Unity. Connection eventually recovered.

- **`prisonerSpawnInterval` field not found via exec compile error**: New field added to GameSettings.cs hadn't compiled yet in exec context. Default value `3f` is set in the source code, so it initializes correctly when Unity compiles.

- **Partially-executed exec causing unexpected changes**: The `Exception has been thrown by the target of an invocation` error caused a partially-run exec that still applied some changes (scale fixes). Verified via follow-up exec that state was actually correct.

- **Miner showing police character**: Root cause was that `main_idle` in `WorkerNPCPrefab` was an instance of `main_idle.fbx` (police character FBX). Fixed by replacing with `miner_Walk.fbx` instance which contains the actual miner mesh (`tripo_node_5282d6c8`) + Mixamo skeleton + animation.

- **Animator T-pose (AutoSell + Worker)**: Animator was on `Visual` parent, but animation clip bone paths (`mixamorig:Hips/...`) are relative to Animator's transform. Bones were under `Visual/main_idle/mixamorig:Hips` → path mismatch → T-pose. Fixed by moving Animator to `main_idle` (the actual FBX root).

5. Problem Solving:

- **Animation path mismatch (root cause of T-pose/no animation)**: Unity Generic animation clips reference bone paths relative to the Animator component's transform. The Animator must be on the FBX root node where bones are direct children. This is a general rule for any new NPC prefab with Mixamo skeleton.

- **Miner character identity**: The miner character mesh is embedded in `miner_Walk.fbx` as `tripo_node_5282d6c8` (SkinnedMeshRenderer). The same FBX provides both the character model AND the walk animation.

- **Worker NPC: stop before rock**: Logic inverted — previously moved to rock position THEN checked for rock. New logic: check rock FIRST, then compute `stopRow` = 1 cell before in approach direction, move to `stopRow`, face rock, mine.

- **Prisoner spawning sequence**: `Start()` previously called `TrySpawn()` twice in a loop (spawning both simultaneously). Fixed to: `TrySpawn()` (spawn 1st immediately) + `StartCoroutine(SpawnWithDelay())` (spawn 2nd after 3 seconds).

**말풍선 폰트**: 30 → 18로 축소

**Miner 걷기**: 행 사이 이동 시 `Speed=0` 중간 정지 제거 → `yield return null`로 바로 다음 행으로 연속 이동

**채굴 애니메이션**: `MiningAnimNotifier`(69%)에서 즉시 종료하던 방식 → `normalizedTime >= 0.97f`까지 대기 후 종료 (전체 1회 재생)

**첫 돌 겹침**: 정지 거리 `spacing * 1.0f` → `spacing * 1.8f`로 증가 (대형 캐릭터 스케일 고려)

**Prisoner 스폰 제한**: 감옥 외부 대기 인원이 4명 이상이면 추가 스폰 차단 (`cellManager.OutsideQueueCount >= 4`)

수정 내용:

**근본 원인**: Walk 클립이 `loop=False`라 1회 재생 후 `normalizedTime=1.0`에 고정 → `IsMining=true` 셋 직후 `norm >= 0.97f` 조건 즉시 충족 → Mining 애니메이션이 재생되기도 전에 탈출

**수정 사항:**
1. `miner_Walk.fbx` — `loopTime=true`로 FBX 재임포트 (걷는 중 애니메이션 끊김 없음)
2. Mining 대기 로직 — `IsMining=true` 설정 후 `animator.GetCurrentAnimatorStateInfo(0).shortNameHash == MiningStateHash`로 실제 Mining 상태 진입을 확인한 뒤 `normalizedTime >= 0.97f`까지 대기 (전체 1회 재생 보장)

[사용자]
지금 문제가 캐는 모션은 나오는데 갔다가 뒤로 이동 채굴이 반복되고 있어 불필요하게 뒤로 이동할 필요 없을 거 같아.

[Claude]
원인이 명확합니다. "돌 없음" 분기에서 `currentRow`의 그리드 중심까지 이동한 뒤, 다음 돌의 `stopPos`가 그 지점보다 뒤에 계산되어 뒷걸음이 발생합니다.

예시:
- row0 채굴 후 miner는 `row0 - 2.16` 위치
- row0 빈 칸 → `row0(=0)`까지 전진 이동
- row1 돌: `stopPos = 1.2 - 2.16 = -0.96` → 현재 위치(0)보다 뒤 → 뒷걸음

**수정: 빈 행에서 이동 없이 row 카운터만 증가**
